Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The monte_carlo variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 705 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 43 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the monte_carlo space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 705 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 43. QED.
